- **memory**: Use for retrieving, organizing, summarizing, saving, revising, or removing durable workspace notes. Trigger when prior project decisions, technical facts, or reusable procedures may answer the current request, or when the user asks to preserve or correct such information. Focus records on project context and operational knowledge, and avoid saving private personal details. Search by meaning or exact wording, use collection names to narrow the scope, and retain source references when they make a note easier to verify later. - **memory-and-gc**: Unreal has **two** memory worlds: garbage-collected `UObject`s managed by the engine's GC, and ordinary C++ heap objects you manage with smart pointers or RAII. Inventories every memory and instruction file the agent loads, splits them into atomic claims, verifies each claim against the current codebase and git history, and reports a per-line verdict table (confirmed, stale, wrong, contradiction, preference) with a receipt for every verdict.
